- **adorno**: agent:adorno — Adorno, the agent that speaks and decides like you. Last step of every workflow that acts in your name (final pass on the reviews you issue, review of your branch and its PR description, verdict and replies on feedback received on your PRs), with your standards, your verification discipline, your severities, your vision and your voice (skills adorno-profile, adorno-voice and the current repo's settings). - **adorno-agent-chore-deps**: agent:chore:deps — Update dependencies — outdated packages, advisories, lockfile drift. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-chore-deps.md))
- **adorno-agent-chore-repo**: agent:chore:repo — Prepare a repo for agents — detect stack and commands, write AGENTS.md and CONSTRAINTS.md, propose the local/adorno.yaml entry. Use the first time a repo is used (workflow:repo:onboard). ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-chore-repo.md))
- **adorno-agent-ci-debug**: agent:ci:debug — Diagnose a red CI from the logs — classify the failure (flaky, infra, real failure) and name the agent that must fix it. Use as soon as a check fails on a PR or on the default branch. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-ci-debug.md))
- **adorno-agent-ci-fix**: agent:ci:fix — Fix the CI pipeline itself — workflow file, job configuration, cache, matrix, pinned action, check script. Use when ci:debug classified a red check as a pipeline problem, not a code or test failure. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-ci-fix.md))
- **adorno-agent-core-analyst**: agent:core:analyst — Analyzes a problem raised in a Slack conversation or a Notion document — what is asked, the context, the probable causes with evidence (code, logs, PRs, issues), what remains unknown, the proposed next step. Read-only. Use in workflow:slack:mentions, workflow:slack:watch and workflow:notion:assigned. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-core-analyst.md))
- **adorno-agent-core-intake**: agent:core:intake — Frames a new code request and turns it into a spec with the permission level — either by discussing (discuss mode), or, when the request or its sources (clear prompt, Slack thread, Notion page, issue, already written plan) are enough, by writing it directly and asking only confirmation questions (confirm mode). Use at the start of any non-trivial request, before any code (workflow:spec:new). ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-core-intake.md))
- **adorno-agent-core-planner**: agent:core:planner — Splits a validated spec into ordered vertical slices, each testable, and picks the family agent that will build each slice. Use after the router, before any implementation. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-core-planner.md))
- **adorno-agent-core-router**: agent:core:router — Picks the workflow pattern (none, sequential, parallel, evaluator-optimizer) from the validated spec and writes run-plan.yaml. Use right after a spec is validated. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-core-router.md))
- **adorno-agent-core-triage**: agent:core:triage — Sorts the review feedback on a PR (category, area, specialist to consult, likely fixer) into triage.json. Never replies itself, agent:adorno rules and replies. Use in workflow:review:received. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-core-triage.md))
- **adorno-agent-docs-write**: agent:docs:write — Write or update documentation — README, guides, API reference, decision records — from the code and the change. Use when a feature, a behavior or a setting changed and its docs must follow. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-docs-write.md))
- **adorno-agent-feat-api**: agent:feat:api — API contracts — REST endpoints, methods, input and output schemas, contracts between modules. Use for a feat slice that creates or extends a public or internal interface. - **adorno-agent-feat-back**: agent:feat:back — Server-side features in any back-end stack — services, entry points (routes, RPC methods, handlers), jobs, database access. Use for a feat slice that touches server logic. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-feat-back.md))
- **adorno-agent-feat-front**: agent:feat:front — Interface-side features — components, pages, state, forms, WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility. Use for a feat slice that touches the UI. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-feat-front.md))
- **adorno-agent-fix-investigate**: agent:fix:investigate — Investigate a bug — reproduce, isolate the cause, return a written diagnosis without changing anything. Use first on a reported bug, an issue or a red CI of unknown cause. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-fix-investigate.md))
- **adorno-agent-fix-patch**: agent:fix:patch — Fix a bug from a diagnosis or a review comment — red test that reproduces, then minimal fix. Use after fix:investigate, or on a review thread classified fix. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-fix-patch.md))
- **adorno-agent-fix-security**: agent:fix:security — Fix a security finding — vulnerable code path, missing authorization, leaked secret or personal data, vulnerable dependency. Use on a finding from review:security or a security report, after the cause is understood. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-fix-security.md))
- **adorno-agent-perf-front**: agent:perf:front — Front-end performance — Core Web Vitals, bundle weight, re-renders, resource loading, interaction latency. Use on a slow page or component, a heavy bundle or a measured web-vitals regression. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-perf-front.md))
- **adorno-agent-perf-mongodb**: agent:perf:mongodb — MongoDB performance — slow queries and aggregations, missing or useless indexes, N+1, projections, volumes. Use on a slow query or screen where the database is suspected. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-perf-mongodb.md))
- **adorno-agent-perf-nodejs**: agent:perf:nodejs — Node.js performance — CPU, memory and leaks, blocked event loop, sequential I/O, costly serialization. Use on a slow or resource-hungry Node.js service or job. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-perf-nodejs.md))
- **adorno-agent-refactor-simplify**: agent:refactor:simplify — Simplify code without changing its behavior — dead code, duplication, needless abstractions, twisted conditions, and review nits. Use on a review comment classified nit or on code judged too complex. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-refactor-simplify.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-api**: agent:review:api — API reviewer — endpoint and method contracts, input validation, error codes and formats, pagination, compatibility, public documentation. Called when a diff touches an interface, or to verify a review comment about an API. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-api.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-back**: agent:review:back — Back-end reviewer — server logic in any stack, entry points (routes, RPC methods, handlers), jobs, errors, concurrency, server-side permissions, performance (unbounded reads, per-item I/O, blocking work in a request path, memory growth). Called when a diff touches the server, or to verify a review comment about the back end. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-back.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-code**: agent:review:code — Code review on five axes (correctness, readability, architecture, security, performance) of a diff or a branch, findings in JSON for merging. Use before any PR is opened and on fix diffs. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-code.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-front**: agent:review:front — Front-end reviewer — components, state, rendering, WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility, design system, web performance (Core Web Vitals, bundle weight, loading, interaction latency). Called when a diff touches the interface, or to verify a review comment about the front end. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-front.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-impact**: agent:review:impact — Measures the real production impact of a merged PR — usage, errors, response time, throughput, resources, database, alerts — by comparing before and after its deployment across all available sources (logs, metrics, traces, errors, analytics, alert channels). Read-only, every number with its source. Use in workflow:pr:impact. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-impact.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-mongodb**: agent:review:mongodb — MongoDB reviewer — queries and aggregations, indexes, N+1, projections, bulk writes, migrations. Called when a diff contains queries or a migration, or to verify a review comment about the database. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-mongodb.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-ponytail**: agent:review:ponytail — Lean review of a diff — code that need not exist, a helper the repo already has, a dependency for a few lines, an unrequested abstraction, a `shortcut:` without limit or trigger. Use alongside review:code on every diff and on added dependencies; also audits a whole repo or a scope of it, read-only (workflow:repo:audit). ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-ponytail.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-security**: agent:review:security — Security audit of a diff — OWASP Top 10, secrets, authentication and authorization, sensitive data, dependencies. Use whenever a diff touches user inputs, auth, data or dependencies. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-security.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-sql**: agent:review:sql — SQL reviewer — relational queries through an ORM or raw SQL (ActiveRecord, Django ORM, database/sql, sqlx, GORM, knex…), N+1, indexes, unbounded reads, injection, migrations that lock a table. Called when a diff contains SQL, ORM queries or a migration on a relational database, or to verify a review comment about it. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-sql.md))
- **adorno-agent-review-tests**: agent:review:tests — Test review of a diff — coverage of risky paths, edge cases, errors, test readability. Use whenever logic changes. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-review-tests.md))
- **adorno-agent-test-e2e**: agent:test:e2e — End-to-end tests and browser QA with evidence — Playwright journeys, screenshots, video and trace attached to the report or the PR. Use to verify a front feature end to end, reproduce a UI bug or provide visual proof of a fix. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-test-e2e.md))
- **adorno-agent-test-unit**: agent:test:unit — Write unit tests for existing logic or requested in review — nominal cases, limits, errors. Use on a review thread classified test or a gap listed by review:tests. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-agent-test-unit.md))
- **adorno-ci-red**: Handles a red CI on a PR — diagnosis from the logs, a single rerun if flaky, otherwise a fix by the designated agent, committed and pushed without asking (workflow:pr:ship), the PR description updated if the scope moved. Use as soon as a check fails. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-ci-red.md))
- **adorno-feature-build**: Take a feature end to end — scoping, routing, sliced plan, build by the feat agents, parallel review, commit and PR without asking (workflow:pr:ship), follow-up until ready to merge. Two human checkpoints, the spec and the merge. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-feature-build.md))
- **adorno-issue-fix**: Fix a bug from a GitHub issue, a Slack thread, a Notion page, a prompt or a validated spec — investigation, red test, minimal fix, review and fix until clean, PR ready for review ("Fixes ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-issue-fix.md))
- **adorno-notion-assigned**: Find the Notion documents assigned to you ("person" properties of the watched databases, status still open) and launch on each new document the agent or workflow that fits — chosen per database in your config, otherwise by the router. Read-only on Notion; report sent to you alone. Modes report (default) or run. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-notion-assigned.md))
- **adorno-pr-impact**: Analyzes the impact of the PRs you merged on a repo — usage, errors, response times, performance and resources compared before / after their deployment across every available source — and posts the report as a comment on each PR. An already reported PR is skipped, one that is too recent waits. Modes report or comment (default). ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-impact.md))
- **adorno-pr-open**: Opens the PR of a ready branch, without asking — title and description in English following your template, in your voice, reviewers from the registry. Ready for review when the review loop of workflow:pr:ship left nothing 🔴 or 🟠, draft otherwise. Default pr_skill of workflow:pr:ship. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-open.md))
- **adorno-pr-review**: Reviews a colleague's PR in a dedicated workspace — specialized reviewers chosen by the touched code areas (front, back, mongodb, api, security…), in parallel, then your agent who rechecks, slices and writes. By default, a pending review that you reread and submit yourself. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-review.md))
- **adorno-pr-ship**: Ends every workflow that touched code — commit (the repo's commit_skill), review and fix until no 🔴 or 🟠 is left and the change meets the request, its proof attached (test output, screenshots and videos of what a user sees), then the PR (the repo's pr_skill) opened ready for review, or pushed and updated when it is already open. No confirmation and no permission prompt, within the run's level. Last step of feature:build, issue:fix, ci:red, review:received and repo:onboard. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-ship.md))
- **adorno-pr-status**: Status of all your open PRs (or those of one repo) — review, CI, unresolved threads, and what blocks each one. Read-only. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-status.md))
- **adorno-pr-watch**: Accompanies an open PR until it is ready to merge — loops on red CI and new review threads, with a cap. The merge stays yours. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-pr-watch.md))
- **adorno-profile-learn**: Evolve your profile and your voice — reviews the points noted by adorno-learn during work (tech, product logic, code logic, method, writing), shows you each with its evidence and the proposed sentence in the right file, and writes only what you validate, in your personal layer. Launched by hand when Adorno signals ready points. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-profile-learn.md))
- **adorno-repo-audit**: Audits a whole repo, read-only — bugs, security holes, what breaks under load, risky code without tests, slow paths, code to delete or merge (skill ponytail-audit) — into a report kept on your machine, never published, public or private repo. Use after an onboarding (workflow:repo:onboard --audit) or every quarter; --area or --path to cover a big repo part by part. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-repo-audit.md))
- **adorno-repo-onboard**: Prepare a repo for the agents — detect stack, commands and checks, write AGENTS.md and CONSTRAINTS.md, propose its complete entry in local/adorno.yaml, then commit them and open the PR without asking (workflow:pr:ship); with --audit, then a read-only audit of the code in its own L0 run, kept on your machine (workflow:repo:audit). Do it once per repo. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-repo-onboard.md))
- **adorno-review-received**: Goes over all your open PRs (or a single one) — every unanswered comment, thread or review summary is checked by the subject's specialist in a dedicated workspace, fixed if needed, then your agent replies in your voice and resolves. Modes report, draft (default), auto. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-review-received.md))
- **adorno-review-requested**: Analyzes all the PRs where your review is requested — a dedicated workspace per PR, specialized reviewers chosen by the touched code, then your agent who slices and writes. By default, a pending review per PR, that you reread and submit yourself. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-review-requested.md))
- **adorno-slack-mentions**: Every time you are mentioned on Slack (on all channels or a list), analyze the problem in the conversation and send you, separately, the full analysis with the link to the conversation and a draft reply in your voice. Once per mention. Read-only on Slack. Launch by hand or from your own scheduler. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-slack-mentions.md))
- **adorno-slack-watch**: Watch one or more Slack channels according to a named rule — included words or regex, over a period, at most N messages — and launch the configured action (agent, workflow or instruction) on each message that matches, once per message. Read-only on Slack; report sent to you alone. Launch by hand or from your own scheduler. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-slack-watch.md))
- **adorno-spec-new**: Scope a code request with the intake agent until you validate — by discussing (discuss), or from an already clear prompt, a Slack thread, a Notion page, an issue or a written plan, asking only the confirmation questions (confirm). First step of any non-trivial feature or fix. ([nicolasmondain/adorno](https://github.com/nicolasmondain/adorno/blob/HEAD/dist/claude-plugin/commands/adorno-spec-new.md))

Si la décision TOUCHE LE MODÈLE LikeC4 : ajoute une section `## Modèle` (éléments touchés en FQN, le delta), édite le .c4 (nouveaux éléments/relations, `extend` si le parent vit dans un autre fichier), ajoute une vue `adr-NNNN of &lt;élément le plus haut&gt;`, valide (likec4 validate --no-layout --json --project), rend la vue en Mermaid (likec4 gen mermaid -o &lt;dir&gt;) et la colle dans l'ADR. Un ADR sans delta… ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-spec-dev/.claude/commands/adr.md))

- **advanced**: Multi-document cross-reference analysis ([davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills](https://github.com/davendra/fundadmin-ai-skills/blob/HEAD/manual/commands/advanced.md))
- **advanced-code-review-context**: **Purpose:** Load historical data from previous reviews, fetch PR context if available, and build the context object for Phase 3. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-context.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-context**: Advanced Code Review Phase 2: Context Analysis - load previous reviews, PR history, declined items ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-context.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-plan**: 1. **Risk-based prioritization**: Higher risk files are reviewed first. Security, payment, and migration files take precedence over tests and documentation. 2. **Scope clarity**: All files in scope must be identified before review starts. No file should be discovered mid-review. 3. **Complexity honesty**: Estimates must reflect actual review effort required. Underestimating leads to rushed reviews ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-plan.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-plan**: Advanced Code Review Phase 1: Strategic Planning - scope analysis, risk categorization, priority ordering ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-plan.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-report**: Advanced Code Review Phase 5: Report Generation - produce final deliverables including Markdown report and JSON summary ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-report.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-report**: 1. **Signal over noise**: Only verified findings appear in the final report. REFUTED findings are excluded. Quality of findings matters more than quantity. 2. **Actionable output**: Every finding must have clear next steps. Findings without suggestions or context are not actionable. 3. **Machine-readable artifacts for automation**: JSON summary enables CI/CD integration, automated triage, and tool ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-report.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-review**: Perform multi-pass code analysis, generate findings with severity classification, and respect previous review context. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-review.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-review**: Advanced Code Review Phase 3: Deep Review - multi-pass code analysis, finding generation with severity classification ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-review.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-verify**: 1. **Every finding must be verifiable against actual code**: If a finding cannot be verified by reading the file at the specified line, it is not a valid finding. 2. **REFUTED findings must be removed, not just flagged**: False positives erode trust. Remove them from final output entirely; log in audit for transparency. 3. **INCONCLUSIVE findings must be clearly marked**: Uncertainty is acceptable ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/advanced-code-review-verify.md) · ★13)
- **advanced-code-review-verify**: Advanced Code Review Phase 4: Verification - fact-check findings against codebase, remove false positives ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/advanced-code-review-verify.md) · ★13)
